Grow the shipped set through the harvest loop, not by bulk-installing a marketplace. ([shepherd-sdlc/shepherd](https://github.com/shepherd-sdlc/shepherd/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/README.md))

- **README**: Project-local Claude Code subagents for the DevDigest repo. Each `*.md` file in this folder defines one subagent: YAML frontmatter (`name`, `description`, `tools`, `model`, optional `skills`) plus a system-prompt body.
